# Skills · R · part 56

900 entries, refactor-seams to refactoring-legacy-code. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **refactor-seams**: Change code that must change but is not safely changeable - find the seams that let you alter behavior without rewriting, using branch-by-abstraction, strangler fig, and adapter insertion, always leaving the system releasable at every step. Use when extracting from a monolith, when a component must be replaced incrementally, when a refactor is too large to do in one change, or when someone proposes a rewrite. Every step ships independently, because a long-lived refactor branch is how refactors die. ([rpraharaj/forward-deployed-engineer](https://github.com/rpraharaj/forward-deployed-engineer/tree/HEAD/fde-migration/skills/refactor-seams) · ★14)
